# Seq-Browser masterlist

Sequencing-based assays, one row per method. Updated 2026-11-10. 516 wet-lab methods, 342 computational (under construction).

**For LLMs / tools:** this file is the complete database. Columns use a fixed vocabulary (see `taxonomy.yaml`). `Variant of` gives the parent method's id (lowercase-hyphenated name). Machine-readable copies: `docs/methods.json`, `docs/methods.csv`. Raw URL: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/RobertT4n/Seq-Browser/main/METHODS.md

## Wet-lab methods

| Method | Aliases | Readouts | Resolution | Temporal | Scope | Chemistry | Lineage | Ref | Cites | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **10x Chromium 5' Gene Expression** |  | rna | single-cell |  | genome-wide | reverse-transcription; droplet-microfluidics | variant of 10x-chromium-single-cell-3-gene-expression; builds on strt-seq | [https://www.10xgenomics.com/support](https://www.10xgenomics.com/support) |  | 5' transcript profiling with a universal RT primer and barcoded template-switching oligos on the gel beads, so reads start at the transcript 5' end. |
| **10x Chromium 5' Immune Profiling Feature Barcoding** |  | rna; protein; immune-repertoire | single-cell |  | genome-wide | reverse-transcription; droplet-microfluidics | variant of 10x-chromium-5-gene-expression; builds on 10x-chromium-single-cell-3-feature-barcoding | [https://www.10xgenomics.com/support](https://www.10xgenomics.com/support) |  | 5' gene expression plus antibody-oligo surface protein tags in one droplet workflow, with V(D)J receptor libraries from the same cells. |
| **10x Chromium Single Cell 3' Feature Barcoding** |  | rna; protein | single-cell |  | genome-wide | reverse-transcription; droplet-microfluidics | builds on 10x-chromium-single-cell-3-gene-expression; cite-seq | [https://www.10xgenomics.com/support](https://www.10xgenomics.com/support) |  | Antibody-oligo tags captured on the same gel beads as mRNA, giving surface-protein counts per droplet alongside transcriptomes. |
| **10x Chromium Single Cell 3' Gene Expression** |  | rna | single-cell |  | genome-wide | reverse-transcription; droplet-microfluidics | builds on drop-seq | [https://www.10xgenomics.com/support](https://www.10xgenomics.com/support) |  | Droplet 3' mRNA counting where gel beads carry cell-barcoded, UMI-tagged oligo-dT primers co-encapsulated with each cell. |
| **10x Chromium Single Cell 3' Solution v1** |  | rna | single-cell |  | genome-wide | reverse-transcription; droplet-microfluidics | builds on drop-seq | Zheng GX 2017, Nat Commun, [doi:10.1038/ncomms14049](https://doi.org/10.1038/ncomms14049) | 7610 | First commercial droplet 3' transcriptome kit, using barcoded gel beads and a microfluidic GemCode device to profile tens of thousands of cells per run. |
| **10x Chromium Single Cell ATAC** | Chromium scATAC | chromatin-accessibility | single-cell |  | genome-wide | tn5; droplet-microfluidics | variant of scatac-seq | Satpathy AT 2019, Nat Biotechnol, [doi:10.1038/s41587-019-0206-z](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41587-019-0206-z) | 1059 | Droplet-based scATAC-seq in which nuclei are tagmented in bulk first, then each tagged nucleus is barcoded in a droplet. |
| **10x Chromium Single Cell Multiome ATAC + Gene Expression** |  | chromatin-accessibility; rna | single-cell |  | genome-wide | tn5; reverse-transcription; droplet-microfluidics | builds on 10x-chromium-single-cell-atac; 10x-chromium-single-cell-3-gene-expression | [https://www.10xgenomics.com/support](https://www.10xgenomics.com/support) |  | Tn5 tagmentation of nuclei followed by droplet capture of tagged open-chromatin DNA and mRNA on the same beads, giving accessibility and expression per nucleus. |
| **10x Visium** | Visium Spatial Gene Expression | rna | spatial |  | genome-wide | reverse-transcription |  | [https://www.10xgenomics.com/products/spatial-gene-expression](https://www.10xgenomics.com/products/spatial-gene-expression) |  | Commercial spatial gene expression assay that captures polyadenylated RNA from tissue sections on barcoded spots on a slide. |

| **3C** (Chromosome conformation capture) |  | 3d-genome | bulk |  | targeted | restriction-digest; proximity-ligation |  | Dekker J 2002, Science, [doi:10.1126/science.1067799](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.1067799) | 3523 | Chromosome conformation capture: crosslink, digest and ligate, then quantify ligation between chosen loci. The ancestor of the C-family. |
| **4C-seq** (Circular chromosome conformation capture sequencing) |  | 3d-genome | bulk |  | targeted | restriction-digest; proximity-ligation | variant of 3c | van de Werken HJ 2012, Nat Methods, [doi:10.1038/nmeth.2173](https://doi.org/10.1038/nmeth.2173) | 397 | Inverse-PCR from a single viewpoint to sequence all of its ligation partners (one-vs-all). |

## Lineage (parent -> child)

`variant` = child is a direct modification of the parent; `builds on` = child combines or draws on the parent.

## Computational methods (under construction - not yet curated)

| Method | Aliases | Readouts | Resolution | Temporal | Scope | Chemistry | Lineage | Ref | Cites | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **ALEA** |  | histone-modification; chromatin-accessibility | bulk |  | genome-wide |  |  | Younesy H 2014, Bioinformatics, [doi:10.1093/bioinformatics/btt744](https://doi.org/10.1093/bioinformatics/btt744) | 20 | Toolbox for allele-specific analysis of epigenomic sequencing data that links allelic variants to histone and chromatin signals. |
| **ALHENA** |  | rna | spatial; single-cell |  | targeted |  |  | Giraud-Sauveur F 2026, bioRxiv, [doi:10.64898/2026.10.20.760439](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.20.760439) |  | H&E image encoder distilled from transcriptomic foundation models trained on paired H&E and Xenium spatial transcriptomics, requiring only H&E at inference. |
| **Alleleauto** |  | genome-sequence; rna | bulk |  | genome-wide |  |  | Shi TL 2026, aBIOTECH, [doi:10.1016/j.abiote.2026.100056](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.abiote.2026.100056) |  | Workflow that identifies true allelic gene pairs in haplotype-resolved diploid assemblies, filtering paralogs by Ks and synteny, and runs allele-specific expression analysis. |
| **AmbientMapper** |  | chromatin-accessibility | single-cell |  | genome-wide |  |  | Gomez-Cano F 2026, bioRxiv, [doi:10.64898/2026.09.11.750809](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.11.750809) |  | Generative model that maps reads competitively against multiple references to estimate and remove ambient chromatin signal. |
| **AmPair** |  | microbial-composition | bulk |  | genome-wide |  |  | Xu X 2026, bioRxiv, [doi:10.64898/2026.08.25.746527](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.08.25.746527) |  | Snakemake pipeline that designs and in silico validates species-level primer pairs for housekeeping-gene amplicon metataxonomics. |
| **AmpliconSuite** |  | genome-sequence | bulk |  | genome-wide |  |  | Luebeck J 2026, Nat Methods, [doi:10.1038/s41592-026-03264-x](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41592-026-03264-x) | 1 | Toolkit that detects and classifies focal amplifications, including extrachromosomal DNA, from cancer whole-genome sequencing. |
| **AnnoAudit** |  | rna | single-cell |  | genome-wide |  |  | Zhang L 2026, bioRxiv, [doi:10.64898/2026.09.03.749125](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.03.749125) |  | Marker-based protocol with four convergent checks and an ambient-aware step that audits single-cell atlas annotations before re-analysis. |
| **ANTIPODE** |  | rna | single-cell |  | genome-wide |  |  | Schmitz MT 2025, bioRxiv, [doi:10.1101/2025.10.18.683238](https://doi.org/10.1101/2025.10.18.683238) | 3 | Deep-learning variational inference framework that integrates single-cell datasets, finds homologous cell types and parcellates differential expression across species. |
| **ASTRO** |  | rna | spatial |  | genome-wide |  |  | Zhang D 2026, Bioinformatics, [doi:10.1093/bioinformatics/btaf688](https://doi.org/10.1093/bioinformatics/btaf688) | 2 | Automated pipeline for whole-transcriptome spatial transcriptomics, optimised for FFPE tissue and non-coding RNAs. |
